   00062: function safeReadLS(key) {
   00063:   try {
   00064:     return localStorage.getItem(key);
   00065:   } catch {
   00066:     return null;
   00067:   }
   00068: }
   00069: 
   00070: function safeWriteLS(key, value) {
   00071:   try {
   00072:     localStorage.setItem(key, value);
   00073:   } catch {}
   00074: }
   00075: 
   00076: function safeRemoveLS(key) {
   00077:   try {
   00078:     localStorage.removeItem(key);
   00079:   } catch {}
   00080: }
   00081: 
   00082: function safeParseJson(raw) {
   00083:   try {
   00084:     return JSON.parse(raw);
   00085:   } catch {
   00086:     return null;
   00087:   }
   00088: }
   00089: 
   00090: function normalizeRoute(saved) {
   00091:   if (!saved) return null;
   00092:   return Object.values(ROUTES).includes(saved) ? saved : null;
   00093: }

   00095: /**
   00096:  * ✅ Resolve default/named de forma robusta
   00097:  */
   00098: function resolveComponent(mod, name) {
   00099:   const c = mod?.default ?? mod;
   00100: 
   00101:   const isProbablyReactComponent =
   00102:     typeof c === "function" ||
   00103:     (c && typeof c === "object" && String(c.$$typeof || "").includes("react."));
   00104: 
   00105:   if (!isProbablyReactComponent) {
   00106:     console.error(`[IMPORT INVALID] ${name} veio inválido:`, c, " | import raw:", mod);
   00107:   }
   00108: 
   00109:   return c;
   00110: }

   00208: function normalizeLoteriaInput(v) {
   00209:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00210:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
   00211: 
   00212:   const key = raw
   00213:     .toLowerCase()
   00214:     .normalize("NFD")
   00215:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   00216:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   00217:     .trim();
   00218: 
   00219:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil") {
   00220:     return "FEDERAL";
   00221:   }
   00222: 
   00223:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio") {
   00224:     return "PT_RIO";
   00225:   }
   00226: 
   00227:   const out = key
   00228:     .toUpperCase()
   00229:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
   00230:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
   00231: 
   00232:   return out || "PT_RIO";
   00233: }
   00234: 
   00235: function loteriaToLotteryKey(loteria) {
   00236:   return normalizeLoteriaInput(loteria);
   00237: }

   00251: function loadDashboardFilters() {

   00257: 
   00258:   const base = getDefaultDashboardFilters();
   00259:   const loteria = normalizeLoteriaInput(obj.loteria);
   00260: 
   00261:   const horario =
   00262:     loteria === "FEDERAL"
   00263:       ? obj.horario === "Todos" || obj.horario === "19h" || obj.horario === "20h"
   00264:         ? obj.horario
   00265:         : "Todos"
   00266:       : typeof obj.horario === "string"
   00267:       ? obj.horario
   00268:       : base.horario;
   00269: 
   00270:   return {
   00271:     loteria,
   00272:     mes: typeof obj.mes === "string" ? obj.mes : base.mes,
   00273:     diaMes: typeof obj.diaMes === "string" ? obj.diaMes : base.diaMes,
   00274:     diaSemana: typeof obj.diaSemana === "string" ? obj.diaSemana : base.diaSemana,

   00355: function cleanPathname(p) {
   00356:   const s = String(p || "").trim();

   00358:   return s.startsWith("/") ? s : `/${s}`;

   00405: function BuildStamp() {
   00406:   const shaShort = BUILD_SHA ? BUILD_SHA.slice(0, 7) : "";
   00407:   const ref = BUILD_REF || "";
   00408:   const tm = BUILD_TIME || "";
   00409:   const text = shaShort
   00410:     ? `build ${shaShort}${ref ? ` · ${ref}` : ""}${tm ? ` · ${tm}` : ""}`
   00411:     : "";
   00412: 
   00413:   if (!shaShort) return null;
   00414: 
   00415:   return (
   00416:     <div
   00417:       style={{
   00418:         position: "fixed",
   00419:         left: 10,
   00420:         bottom: 10,
   00421:         zIndex: 99999,
   00422:         padding: "7px 10px",
   00423:         borderRadius: 999,
   00424:         background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.62)",
   00425:         border: "1px solid rgba(202,166,75,0.28)",
   00426:         color: "rgba(233,233,233,0.88)",
   00427:         fontSize: 11,
   00428:         fontWeight: 900,
   00429:         letterSpacing: 0.2,
   00430:         boxShadow: "0 14px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.40)",
   00431:         userSelect: "text",
   00432:       }}
   00433:       title={`SHA=${BUILD_SHA}${ref ? ` | ref=${ref}` : ""}${tm ? ` | time=${tm}` : ""}`}
   00434:     >
   00435:       {text}
   00436:     </div>
   00437:   );
   00438: }

   00010: function isYmdStrict(s) {
   00011:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
   00012:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
   00013:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
   00014:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00015:   return (
   00016:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
   00017:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
   00018:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
   00019:   );
   00020: }
   00021:

   00034:     if (vite) return vite === "1" || vite.toLowerCase() === "true";
   00035:   } catch {}

   00044: function dayDiffInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo) {
   00045:   const toUTC = (ymd) => {
   00046:     const m = String(ymd).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00047:     if (!m) return null;
   00048:     return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00049:   };

   00052:   const db = toUTC(ymdTo);
   00053:   if (!da || !db) return NaN;

   00056:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return NaN;
   00057:

   00059: }

   00001: // src/pages/Account/account.session.js
   00002: 
   00003: /**
   00004:  * Sessão global do app (pp_session_v1)
>> 00005:  * - Fonte de verdade usada pelo App.jsx para decidir login vs dashboard
   00006:  * - Dispara evento "pp_session_changed" no mesmo tab
   00007:  */
   00008: 
   00009: import { ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY, SESSION_CHANGED_EVENT } from "./account.constants";
   00010: 
   00011: function hasWindow() {
   00012:   return typeof window !== "undefined";
   00013: }
   00014: 
   00015: function canUseLocalStorage() {
   00016:   if (!hasWindow()) return false;
   00017:   try {
   00018:     const ls = window.localStorage;
   00019:     if (!ls) return false;
   00020: 
   00021:     // probe rápido para garantir que não é bloqueado (Safari private / políticas)
   00022:     const k = "__pp_ls_probe__";
   00023:     ls.setItem(k, "1");
   00024:     ls.removeItem(k);
   00025:     return true;
   00026:   } catch {
   00027:     return false;
   00028:   }
   00029: }
   00030: 
   00031: /* =========================
   00032:    Event
   00033: ========================= */
   00034: 
   00035: export function dispatchSessionChanged() {
   00036:   try {
   00037:     if (!hasWindow()) return;
   00038:     window.dispatchEvent(new Event(SESSION_CHANGED_EVENT));
   00039:   } catch {}
   00040: }
   00041: 
   00042: /* =========================
   00043:    Storage
   00044: ========================= */
   00045: 
   00046: export function safeWriteSession(obj) {
   00047:   try {
   00048:     if (!canUseLocalStorage()) return;
   00049:     window.localStorage.setItem(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(obj));
   00050:   } catch {}
   00051:   dispatchSessionChanged();
   00052: }
   00053: 
   00054: export function safeRemoveSession() {
   00055:   try {
   00056:     if (!canUseLocalStorage()) return;
   00057:     window.localStorage.removeItem(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY);
   00058:   } catch {}
   00059:   dispatchSessionChanged();
   00060: }
   00061: 
   00062: /* =========================
   00063:    Plan helpers
   00064: ========================= */
   00065: 
   00066: function normalizePlan(plan) {
   00067:   const raw = String(plan ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
   00068: 
   00069:   if (raw === "VIP") return "VIP";
   00070:   if (raw === "PREMIUM") return "PREMIUM";
   00071:   if (raw === "FREE") return "FREE";
   00072:   return "";
   00073: }
   00074:

   00018: function pad2(value) {
   00019:   return String(value).padStart(2, "0");
   00020: }
   00021: 
   00022: function isYmd(value) {
   00023:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(
   00024:     String(value || "").trim()
   00025:   );
   00026: }
   00027: 
   00028: function ymdToUtc(value) {
   00029:   if (!isYmd(value)) return null;
   00030: 
   00031:   const [year, month, day] = value
   00032:     .split("-")
   00033:     .map(Number);
   00034: 
   00035:   return new Date(
   00036:     Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day)
   00037:   );
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: function addDays(value, amount) {
   00041:   const date = ymdToUtc(value);
   00042: 
   00043:   if (!date) return value;
   00044: 
   00045:   date.setUTCDate(
   00046:     date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0)
   00047:   );
   00048: 
   00049:   return [
   00050:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
   00051:     pad2(date.getUTCMonth() + 1),
   00052:     pad2(date.getUTCDate()),
   00053:   ].join("-");
   00054: }
   00055: 
   00056: function splitRange(dateFrom, dateTo) {
   00057:   const chunks = [];
   00058: 
   00059:   if (!isYmd(dateFrom) || !isYmd(dateTo)) {
   00060:     return chunks;
   00061:   }
   00062: 
   00063:   let cursor = dateFrom;
   00064: 
   00065:   while (cursor <= dateTo) {
   00066:     const candidateTo = addDays(
   00067:       cursor,
   00068:       CHUNK_DAYS - 1
   00069:     );
   00070: 
   00071:     const chunkTo =
   00072:       candidateTo > dateTo
   00073:         ? dateTo
   00074:         : candidateTo;
   00075: 
   00076:     chunks.push({
   00077:       from: cursor,
   00078:       to: chunkTo,
   00079:     });
   00080: 
   00081:     cursor = addDays(chunkTo, 1);
   00082:   }
   00083: 
   00084:   return chunks;
   00085: }
   00086: 
   00087: function firstValidYmd(...values) {

   00089:     const normalized = String(
   00090:       value || ""
   00091:     ).trim();
   00092: 
   00093:     if (isYmd(normalized)) {
   00094:       return normalized;
   00095:     }
   00096:   }
   00097: 
   00098:   return "";
   00099: }

   00383: function formatScore(value) {

   00385:

   00393:   });

   00396: function formatPercent(value) {

   00404:     "pt-BR",
   00405:     {

   00407:       maximumFractionDigits: 2,

   00057: function pad2(n) {
   00058:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00059: }
   00060: function isYMD(s) {
   00061:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00062: }
   00063: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00064:   const m = String(ymd || "").trim().match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00065:   if (!m) return "";
   00066:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00067: }
   00068: function ymdToUTCDate(ymd) {
   00069:   const m = String(ymd || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00070:   if (!m) return null;
   00071:   return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00072: }

   00056: function safeParseJSON(s) {
   00057:   try {
   00058:     const obj = JSON.parse(s);
   00059:     return obj && typeof obj === "object" ? obj : null;
   00060:   } catch {
   00061:     return null;
   00062:   }
   00063: }
   00064: 
   00065: function loadSessionObj() {
   00066:   try {
   00067:     const raw = localStorage.getItem(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY);
   00068:     if (!raw) return null;
   00069:     const s = String(raw || "").trim();
   00070:     if (!s.startsWith("{")) return null;
   00071:     return safeParseJSON(s);
   00072:   } catch {
   00073:     return null;
   00074:   }
   00075: }
   00076: 
   00077: function normalizeSessionPlan(plan) {
   00078:   const p = String(plan || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   00079:   if (p === "VIP") return "VIP";
   00080:   if (p === "PREMIUM") return "PREMIUM";
   00081:   if (p === "FREE") return "FREE";
   00082:   return "";
   00083: }
   00084: 
   00085: function getSessionKind(sess) {
   00086:   const s = sess || loadSessionObj();
   00087:   if (!s || s.ok !== true) return "anon";
   00088: 
   00089:   const type = String(s.type || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   00090:   if (type === "guest") return "guest";
   00091:   if (type === "user") return "user";
   00092: 
   00093:   const loginType = String(s.loginType || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   00094:   const loginId = String(s.loginId || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   00095:   const mode = String(s.mode || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   00096: 
   00097:   if (
   00098:     loginType === "guest" ||
   00099:     loginId === "guest" ||
   00100:     s.skipped === true ||
   00101:     mode === "skip"
   00102:   ) {
   00103:     return "guest";
   00104:   }
   00105: 
   00106:   if (loginType === "user" || loginId === "user") {
   00107:     return "user";
   00108:   }
   00109: 
   00110:   if (s.uid || s.email) return "user";
   00111: 
   00112:   return "anon";
   00113: }

   00030: function clamp(n, a, b) {
   00031:   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   00032: }
   00033: 
   00034: function pad2(n) {
   00035:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00036: }
   00037: 
   00038: /** ✅ Normaliza "qualquer data" para YYYY-MM-DD (ou null). */
   00039: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   00040:   if (!input) return null;
   00041: 
   00042:   // Firestore Timestamp com toDate()
   00043:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   00044:     const d = input.toDate();
   00045:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00046:       return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
   00047:         d.getUTCDate()
   00048:       )}`;
   00049:     }
   00050:   }
   00051: 
   00052:   // Timestamp-like { seconds } / { _seconds }
   00053:   if (
   00054:     typeof input === "object" &&
   00055:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ||
   00056:       Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
   00057:   ) {
   00058:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
   00059:       ? Number(input.seconds)
   00060:       : Number(input._seconds);
   00061: 
   00062:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
   00063:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00064:       return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
   00065:         d.getUTCDate()
   00066:       )}`;
   00067:     }
   00068:   }
   00069: 
   00070:   // Date

   00031: function pad2(n) {
   00032:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00033: }
   00034: 
   00035: function isISODate(s) {
   00036:   return /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.test(String(s || ""));
   00037: }
   00038: 
   00039: function formatBR(iso) {
   00040:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return "";
   00041:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-");
   00042:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
   00043: }
   00044: 
   00045: function toUTCDate(iso) {
   00046:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return null;
   00047:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
   00048:   if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
   00049:   return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00050: }
   00051: 
   00052: function fromUTCDate(dt) {
   00053:   if (!(dt instanceof Date) || !Number.isFinite(dt.getTime())) return null;
   00054:   const y = dt.getUTCFullYear();
   00055:   const m = pad2(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1);
   00056:   const d = pad2(dt.getUTCDate());
   00057:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: function clamp(n, a, b) {
   00061:   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   00062: }
   00063: 
   00064: function clampDateISO(iso, minISO, maxISO) {
   00065:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   00066:   const minDt = toUTCDate(minISO);
   00067:   const maxDt = toUTCDate(maxISO);
   00068: 
   00069:   if (!minDt || !maxDt) return isISODate(iso) ? iso : null;
   00070:   if (!dt) return maxISO;
   00071: 
   00072:   const clamped = new Date(clamp(dt.getTime(), minDt.getTime(), maxDt.getTime()));
   00073:   return fromUTCDate(clamped) || maxISO;
   00074: }
   00075: 
   00076: function daysBetweenUTC(aISO, bISO) {
   00077:   const a = toUTCDate(aISO);
   00078:   const b = toUTCDate(bISO);
   00079:   if (!a || !b) return 0;
   00080:   const ms = b.getTime() - a.getTime();
   00081:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return 0;
   00082:   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000);
   00083: }
   00084: 
   00085: function addDaysUTC(iso, days) {
   00086:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   00087:   if (!dt) return iso;
   00088:   dt.setUTCDate(dt.getUTCDate() + Number(days || 0));
   00089:   return fromUTCDate(dt);
   00090: }

   00034: }

   00036: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   00037:   if (!input) return null;
   00038: 
   00039:   // Firestore Timestamp-like: { toDate() }
   00040:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   00041:     const d = input.toDate();
   00042:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00043:       return ymdFromDateUTC(d);
   00044:     }
   00045:   }
   00046: 
   00047:   // Timestamp-like: { seconds } / { _seconds }
   00048:   if (
   00049:     typeof input === "object" &&
   00050:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) || Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
   00051:   ) {
   00052:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
   00053:       ? Number(input.seconds)
   00054:       : Number(input._seconds);
   00055: 
   00056:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
   00057:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00058:       return ymdFromDateUTC(d);
   00059:     }
   00060:   }
   00061: 
   00062:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
   00063:     return ymdFromDateUTC(input);
   00064:   }
   00065: 
   00066:   const s = String(input).trim();
   00067:   if (!s) return null;
   00068: 
   00069:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
   00070:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;

   00008: function buildSlots(digits) {
   00009:   const d = String(digits || "").slice(0, 4);
   00010:   const len = d.length;
   00011: 
   00012:   // antes de 2 dígitos: só mostra “vazio” (bolinhas)
   00013:   if (len < 2) return ["", "", "", ""];
   00014: 
   00015:   const slots = ["", "", "", ""];
   00016: 
   00017:   // 2 dígitos = dezena (últimos 2) => **DD
   00018:   if (len === 2) {
   00019:     slots[0] = "*";
   00020:     slots[1] = "*";
   00021:     slots[2] = d[0];
   00022:     slots[3] = d[1];
   00023:     return slots;
   00024:   }
   00025: 
   00026:   // 3 dígitos = centena (últimos 3) => *DDD
   00027:   if (len === 3) {
   00028:     slots[0] = "*";
   00029:     slots[1] = d[0];
   00030:     slots[2] = d[1];
   00031:     slots[3] = d[2];
   00032:     return slots;
   00033:   }
   00034: 
   00035:   // 4 dígitos = milhar => DDDD
   00036:   slots[0] = d[0];
   00037:   slots[1] = d[1];
   00038:   slots[2] = d[2];
   00039:   slots[3] = d[3];
   00040:   return slots;
   00041: }

   00018: function cx(...parts) {
   00019:   return parts.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
   00020: }
   00021: 
   00022: function pad2(n) {
   00023:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00024: }
   00025: 
   00026: function safeStr(v) {
   00027:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00028: }
   00029: 
   00030: function isYMD(s) {
   00031:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00032: }
   00033: 
   00034: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00035:   const m = String(ymd || "").trim().match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00036:   if (!m) return safeStr(ymd);
   00037:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00041:   const s0 = safeStr(value);
   00042:   if (!s0) return "";
   00043: 
   00044:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00045: 
   00046:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00047:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00048: 
   00049:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00050:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00051: 
   00052:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00053:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00054: 
   00055:   return s0;
   00056: }
   00057: 
   00058: function toHourBucket(hhmm) {
   00059:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
   00060:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00061:   if (!m) return s;
   00062:   return `${m[1]}:00`;
   00063: }
   00064: 
   00065: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   00066:   if (!input) return null;
   00067: 
   00068:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   00069:     const d = input.toDate();
   00070:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00071:       return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00072:     }

   00209: function clampYmd(ymd, minDate, maxDate) {
   00210:   const d = normalizeToYMD(ymd);
   00211:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
   00212: 
   00213:   let out = d;
   00214: 
   00215:   if (isYMD(minDate) && out < minDate) out = minDate;
   00216:   if (isYMD(maxDate) && out > maxDate) out = maxDate;
   00217: 
   00218:   return out;
   00219: }
   00220: 
   00221: function normalizeRangeWithBounds(fromIn, toIn, minDate, maxDate) {
   00222:   let from = clampYmd(fromIn, minDate, maxDate);
   00223:   let to = clampYmd(toIn, minDate, maxDate);
   00224: 
   00225:   const fallback = clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minDate, maxDate) || maxDate || minDate || todayYMDLocal();
   00226: 
   00227:   if (!from) from = fallback;
   00228:   if (!to) to = from;
   00229: 
   00230:   if (from > to) [from, to] = [to, from];
   00231: 
   00232:   return { from, to };
   00233: }

   00025:  * ✅ IMPORTANTE (SEU PROJETO):
   00026:  * - Consultas devem usar UF="RJ" (com trava interna para PT_RIO)

   00028: 
   00029: function pad2(n) {
   00030:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00031: }
   00032: 
   00033: function safeStr(v) {
   00034:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00035: }
   00036: 
   00037: function todayYMDLocal() {
   00038:   const d = new Date();
   00039:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00040: }
   00041: 
   00042: function isYMD(s) {
   00043:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00044: }
   00045: 
   00046: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00047:   const m = String(ymd || "")
   00048:     .trim()
   00049:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00050:   if (!m) return "";
   00051:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00052: }
   00053: 
   00054: function ymdToUTCDate(ymd) {
   00055:   const m = String(ymd || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00056:   if (!m) return null;
   00057:   return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: function diffDaysUTC(aYmd, bYmd) {
   00061:   const a = ymdToUTCDate(aYmd);
   00062:   const b = ymdToUTCDate(bYmd);
   00063:   if (!a || !b) return NaN;
   00064:   return Math.floor((b.getTime() - a.getTime()) / 86400000);
   00065: }
   00066: 
   00067: function toGrupo2(n) {
   00068:   const v = Number(n);
   00069:   if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return "";
   00070:   return pad2(v);
   00071: }
   00072: 
   00073: function isValidGrupo(n) {
   00074:   const v = Number(n);
   00075:   return Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 1 && v <= 25;
   00076: }
   00077: 
   00078: function sortPTBR(a, b) {
   00079:   return String(a || "").localeCompare(String(b || ""), "pt-BR", { sensitivity: "base" });
   00080: }
   00081: 
   00082: /**
   00083:  * Normaliza entradas como:
   00084:  * "09h" / "09HS" / "9" / "09:10" -> "09:00" ou "09:10"
   00085:  */
   00086: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00087:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00088:   if (!s0) return "";
   00089: 
   00090:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00091:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00092:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;

   00245:   const d = safeStr(ymd);
   00246:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
   00247: 
   00248:   let out = d;
>> 00249:   const min = safeStr(minYmd);
>> 00250:   const max = safeStr(maxYmd);
   00251: 
   00252:   if (isYMD(min) && out < min) out = min;
   00253:   if (isYMD(max) && out > max) out = max;
   00254: 
   00255:   return out;
   00256: }
   00257: 
>> 00258: function normalizeSingleDateWithBounds(dateIn, minYmd, maxYmd) {
>> 00259:   const d = clampYmd(dateIn, minYmd, maxYmd);
   00260:   if (d) return d;
   00261: 
   00262:   const fallback =
>> 00263:     clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minYmd, maxYmd) ||
>> 00264:     (isYMD(maxYmd) ? maxYmd : null) ||
>> 00265:     (isYMD(minYmd) ? minYmd : null) ||
   00266:     todayYMDLocal();
   00267: 
   00268:   return fallback;
   00269: }

   00280: }

   00019: function pad2(n) {
   00020:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00021: }
   00022: 
   00023: function safeStr(v) {
   00024:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00025: }
   00026: 
   00027: function isYMD(s) {
   00028:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00029: }
   00030: 
   00031: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00032:   const m = String(ymd || "")
   00033:     .trim()
   00034:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00035:   if (!m) return safeStr(ymd);
   00036:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00037: }
   00038: 
   00039: function nowLocal() {
   00040:   return new Date();
   00041: }
   00042: 
   00043: function todayYMDLocal() {
   00044:   const d = nowLocal();
   00045:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00046: }
   00047: 
   00048: function currentHourNumLocal() {
   00049:   const d = nowLocal();
   00050:   return d.getHours() * 100 + d.getMinutes();
   00051: }
   00052: 
   00053: function ymdToDateLocal(ymd) {
   00054:   const m = String(ymd || "")
   00055:     .trim()
   00056:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00057:   if (!m) return null;
   00058:   return new Date(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3]), 12, 0, 0);
   00059: }
   00060: 
   00061: function dateToYMDLocal(d) {
   00062:   if (!(d instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "";
   00063:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00064: }
   00065: 
   00066: function startOfMonthYMD(ymd) {
   00067:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
   00068:   if (!d) return todayYMDLocal().slice(0, 8) + "01";
   00069:   d.setDate(1);
   00070:   return dateToYMDLocal(d);
   00071: }
   00072: 
   00073: function endOfMonthYMD(ymd) {
   00074:   const d = ymdToDateLocal(ymd);
   00075:   if (!d) return todayYMDLocal();
   00076:   d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + 1, 0);
   00077:   return dateToYMDLocal(d);

   00010: /**
   00011:  * Search — Premium (SEM índice)
   00012:  * - 2 dígitos => dezena (últimos 2)  ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00013:  * - 3 dígitos => centena (últimos 3) ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00014:  * - 4 dígitos => milhar (4)         ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00015:  *
   00016:  * ✅ REGRA GLOBAL DO PROJETO:
   00017:  * - 7º prêmio = CENTENA (3 dígitos) e NÃO pode virar 4 dígitos.
   00018:  * - Nenhum zero à esquerda pode ser ignorado.
   00019:  *
   00020:  * ✅ UI:
   00021:  * - Mantém apenas 1 card, com 1 bloco: "OCORRÊNCIAS"
   00022:  *
   00023:  * ✅ PERF:
   00024:  * - Busca em chunks maiores (60 dias) + concorrência limitada
   00025:  * - Renderiza resultados de forma incremental
   00026:  */
   00027: 
   00028: function pad2(n) {
   00029:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00030: }
   00031: 
   00032: function safeStr(v) {
   00033:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00034: }
   00035: 
   00036: function isYMD(s) {
   00037:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: /* =========================
>> 00041:    Bounds helpers (robusto)

   00044: function normalizeBoundsResponse(b) {
>> 00045:   const minRaw = safeStr(b?.minYmd || b?.minDate || b?.min || "");
>> 00046:   const maxRaw = safeStr(b?.maxYmd || b?.maxDate || b?.max || "");
>> 00047:   const minYmd = isYMD(minRaw) ? minRaw : null;
>> 00048:   const maxYmd = isYMD(maxRaw) ? maxRaw : null;
>> 00049:   return { minYmd, maxYmd, source: safeStr(b?.source || "") };
   00050: }
   00051: 
>> 00052: function clampYmd(ymd, minYmd, maxYmd) {
   00053:   const d = safeStr(ymd);
   00054:   if (!isYMD(d)) return "";
   00055:   let out = d;
   00056: 
>> 00057:   const min = safeStr(minYmd);
>> 00058:   const max = safeStr(maxYmd);
   00059: 
   00060:   if (isYMD(min) && out < min) out = min;
   00061:   if (isYMD(max) && out > max) out = max;
   00062: 
   00063:   return out;
   00064: }
   00065: 
   00066: function normalizeDigitsOnly(v) {
   00067:   return String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00068: }
   00069: 
   00070: /**
   00071:  * ✅ Extrai os ÚLTIMOS N dígitos e preserva zero à esquerda.
   00072:  * - Não converte para Number em nenhum momento.
   00073:  */
   00074: function extractLastNDigits(v, n) {
   00075:   const digits = normalizeDigitsOnly(v);
   00076:   if (!digits) return null;
   00077:   const last = digits.slice(-n).padStart(n, "0");
   00078:   const re = new RegExp(`^\\d{${n}}$`);
   00079:   return re.test(last) ? last : null;
   00080: }
   00081: 
   00082: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00083:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00084:   if (!s0) return "";
   00085:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00086: 
   00087:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00088:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00089: 
   00090:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00091:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00092: 
   00093:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00094:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00095: 
   00096:   return s0.trim();
   00097: }
   00098: 
   00099: // Bucket "09h", "11h"…
   00100: function toHourBucket(value) {
   00101:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00102:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
   00103: 
   00104:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   00105:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}h`;
   00106: 
   00107:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00108:   if (m1) return `${pad2(m1[1])}h`;
   00109: 
   00110:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00111:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}h`;
   00112: 
   00113:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00114:   if (m3) return `${pad2(m3[1])}h`;
   00115: 
   00116:   return null;
   00117: }
   00118: 
   00119: // Dia da semana por UTC
   00120: function dowFromYmdUTC(ymd) {
   00121:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return "";
   00122:   const [y, m, d] = ymd.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   00123:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00124:   const dow = dt.getUTCDay();
   00125:   const labels = ["Dom", "Seg", "Ter", "Qua", "Qui", "Sex", "Sáb"];
   00126:   return labels[dow] || "";
   00127: }

   00490: /* =========================
   00491:    Pool (concorrência limitada)
   00492: ========================= */
   00493: 
   00494: async function runPool(items, worker, concurrency = 3) {
   00495:   const queue = [...items];
   00496:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, concurrency) }, async () => {
   00497:     while (queue.length) {
   00498:       const it = queue.shift();
   00499:       // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
   00500:       await worker(it);
   00501:     }
   00502:   });
   00503:   await Promise.all(runners);
   00504: }

   00276: export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
   00277:   draws,
   00278:   targetHourBucket,
   00279:   schedule
   00280: ) {
   00281:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   00282:   if (!list.length) return null;
   00283: 
   00284:   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
   00285:   if (t < 0) return null;
   00286: 
   00287:   const candidates = list
   00288:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
   00289:     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
   00290:     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
   00291:     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
   00292: 
   00293:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
   00294: }

   00283:

   00285:   if (t < 0) return null;

   00291:     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
   00292: 
   00293:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
   00294: }

   00228:  * - quarta-feira às 20h;

   00232: export function isFederalDrawDay(ymd) {
   00233:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   00234:   if (!y) return false;
   00235: 
   00236:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
   00237: 
   00238:   if (y >= FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD) {
   00239:     return dow === 0 || dow === 3;
   00240:   }
   00241: 
   00242:   return dow === 3 || dow === 6;
   00243: }
   00244: 
   00245: function getFederalScheduleForYmd(ymd, FEDERAL_SCHEDULE) {
   00246:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   00247: 
   00248:   if (!y || !isFederalDrawDay(y)) {
   00249:     return [];
   00250:   }
   00251: 
   00252:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
   00253: 
   00254:   if (y >= FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD && dow === 0) {

   00271:     ? normalized
   00272:     : ["20:00"];

   00275: /**
   00276:  * Determina apenas os slots esperados.

   00279: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
   00280:   ymd,
   00281:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   00282:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   00283: ) {
   00284:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   00285:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
   00286: 
   00287:   if (dow === 0) {
   00288:     const sundaySchedule = Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY)
   00289:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY]
   00290:       : [];
   00291: 
   00292:     if (y >= PT_RIO_SUNDAY_REDUCED_START_YMD) {
   00293:       return sundaySchedule.filter((hour) => {
   00294:         const h = toHourBucket(hour);
   00295: 
   00296:         return h === "14:00" || h === "16:00";
   00297:       });
   00298:     }
   00299: 
   00300:     return sundaySchedule;
   00301:   }
   00302: 
   00303:   if (dow === 6) {
   00304:     const saturdaySchedule = Array.isArray(
   00305:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   00306:     )
   00307:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
   00308:       : [];
   00309: 
   00310:     if (y >= "2026-07-18") {
   00311:       const transitionedSchedule = saturdaySchedule
   00312:         .map(toHourBucket)
   00313:         .filter(Boolean)
   00314:         .filter((hour) => hour !== "18:00");
   00315: 
   00316:       if (!transitionedSchedule.includes("19:00")) {
   00317:         transitionedSchedule.push("19:00");
   00318:       }
   00319: 
   00320:       return transitionedSchedule
   00321:         .filter(
   00322:           (hour, index, schedule) =>
   00323:             schedule.indexOf(hour) === index
   00324:         )
   00325:         .sort(
   00326:           (hourA, hourB) =>
   00327:             hourToInt(hourA) - hourToInt(hourB)
   00328:         );
   00329:     }
   00330: 
   00331:     return saturdaySchedule;

   00085: function drawTs(draw) {
   00086:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   00087:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   00088: 
   00089:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
   00090: 
   00091:   const [Y, M, D] = String(y).split("-").map(Number);
   00092:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
   00093: 
   00094:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
   00095:     return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
   00096:   }
   00097: 
   00098:   return Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
   00099: }
   00100: 
   00101: function drawKey(draw) {
   00102:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   00103:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   00104:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "";
   00105: }
   00106: 
   00107: function hasDrawAtHour(draws, hourBucket) {

   00112:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   00113:     return h === target;
   00114:   });
   00115: }

   00091:           item.frequencyRate

   00097:     module: "frequency",

   00103:       percent,
   00104:       level,
   00105:       totalUniverse,
   00106:

   00108:       lotteryKey: context.lotteryKey || null,

   00110:       weekday: context.weekday || null,
   00111:       hour: context.hour || null,
   00112:     },
   00113:

   00385: /**
   00386:  * AUDIT MASTER (resumo de integridade)

   00388: export function auditDatasetSummary(drawsRaw, options = {}) {
   00389:   const draws = dedupeDraws(drawsRaw);
   00390:   const sampleLimit = Number(options?.sampleLimit ?? 20);
   00391: 
   00392:   let drawsWithPrizes = 0;
   00393:   let drawsWithoutPrizes = 0;
   00394: 
   00395:   let prizesTotal = 0;
   00396: 
   00397:   let invalidDateCount = 0;
   00398:   let invalidCloseHourCount = 0;
   00399: 
   00400:   let invalidGrupoCount = 0;
   00401:   let missingGrupoCount = 0;
   00402: 
   00403:   let missingPositionCount = 0;
   00404:   let invalidPositionCount = 0;
   00405: 
   00406:   for (const d of draws) {
   00407:     const prizes = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
   00408: 
   00409:     if (prizes.length) drawsWithPrizes += 1;
   00410:     else drawsWithoutPrizes += 1;
   00411: 
   00412:     const ymd = normalizeToYMD(extractRawDateFromDraw(d));
   00413:     if (!ymd) invalidDateCount += 1;
   00414: 
   00415:     const hhmm = normalizeHourLike(extractRawCloseHourFromDraw(d));
   00416:     if (!hhmm || !isValidHHMM(hhmm)) invalidCloseHourCount += 1;
   00417: 
   00418:     prizesTotal += prizes.length;
   00419: 
   00420:     for (const p of prizes) {
   00421:       const gNum = Number(p?.grupo ?? p?.group);
   00422:       if (!Number.isFinite(gNum)) {
   00423:         missingGrupoCount += 1;
   00424:       } else if (gNum < 1 || gNum > 25) {
   00425:         invalidGrupoCount += 1;
   00426:       }
   00427: 
   00428:       const posRaw = p?.posicao ?? p?.position;
   00429:       if (posRaw === null || posRaw === undefined || posRaw === "") {
   00430:         missingPositionCount += 1;
   00431:       } else {
   00432:         const pos = Number(posRaw);
   00433:         if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 10) {
   00434:           invalidPositionCount += 1;
   00435:         }
   00436:       }
   00437:     }
   00438:   }
   00439: 
   00440:   const byGrupo = auditCountsByGrupo(draws, {
   00441:     position: options?.position ?? null,
   00442:     hour: options?.hour ?? null,
   00443:     matchHourOnly: options?.matchHourOnly ?? false,
   00444:   });
   00445: 
   00446:   const animalMismatchCount = byGrupo.integrityIssues.length;
   00447:   const integrityIssuesSample = byGrupo.integrityIssues.slice(
   00448:     0,
   00449:     Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(sampleLimit) ? sampleLimit : 20)
   00450:   );
   00451: 
   00452:   const totals = computeDrawTotals(draws);
   00453: 
   00454:   return {
   00455:     totals: {
   00456:       draws: draws.length,
>> 00457:       uniqueDays: totals.uniqueDays,
   00458:       drawsWithPrizes,
   00459:       drawsWithoutPrizes,
   00460:       prizesTotal,
   00461:     },
   00462:     issues: {
   00463:       invalidDateCount,
   00464:       invalidCloseHourCount,
   00465:       missingGrupoCount,
   00466:       invalidGrupoCount,
   00467:       missingPositionCount,
   00468:       invalidPositionCount,
   00469:       animalMismatchCount,
   00470:     },
   00471:     byGrupo: {
   00472:       rowsByGrupo: byGrupo.rowsByGrupo,
   00473:       rowsSorted: byGrupo.rows,
   00474:       totalOcorrencias: byGrupo.totalOcorrencias,
   00475:       appliedFilters: byGrupo.appliedFilters,
   00476:     },
   00477:     integrityIssuesSample,
   00478:     auditOptions: {
   00479:       position: options?.position ?? null,
   00480:       hour: options?.hour ?? null,
   00481:       matchHourOnly: Boolean(options?.matchHourOnly),
   00482:       sampleLimit: Number.isFinite(sampleLimit) ? sampleLimit : 20,

   00484:   };

   00089: /**
   00090:  * ✅ Regra histórica da Federal
   00091:  * - até 2025-11-02: 19h
   00092:  * - a partir de 2025-11-03: 20h
   00093:  */
   00094: export function getFederalPreferredHourForYmd(ymd) {
   00095:   const y = String(ymd || "").trim();
   00096:   if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(y) && y >= FEDERAL_19H_TO_20H_CHANGE_YMD) {
   00097:     return "20:00";
   00098:   }
   00099:   return "19:00";
   00100: }

   00106: /**
   00107:  * Detecta se o usuário está pedindo Federal (por input/alias).
   00108:  */
   00109: function isFederalInput(scopeInput) {
   00110:   const up = String(scopeInput || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   00111:   if (!up) return false;
   00112:   if (up === FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE) return true;
   00113:   if (FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(up)) return true;
   00114: 
   00115:   // normaliza espaços/underscores pra captar variações
   00116:   const compact = up.replace(/[\s_]+/g, " ").trim();
   00117:   return (
   00118:     FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(compact) ||
   00119:     FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(up.replace(/[\s_]+/g, "_"))
   00120:   );
   00121: }
   00122: 
   00123: /**
   00124:  * Resolve qual lottery_key usar quando o usuário passar:
   00125:  * - "RJ" -> "PT_RIO"
   00126:  * - "FEDERAL"/aliases -> "FEDERAL" (canônico)
   00127:  * - outros -> mantém o valor original (compatível com seu app atual)
   00128:  */
   00129: function resolveLotteryKeyForQuery(ufInput) {
   00130:   const uf = String(ufInput || "").trim();
   00131:   if (!uf) return "";
   00132:   const up = uf.toUpperCase();
   00133: 
   00134:   if (up === RJ_STATE_CODE) return RJ_LOTTERY_KEY;
   00135:   if (isFederalInput(up)) return FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE;
   00136: 
   00137:   return uf;
   00138: }
   00139: 
   00140: /**
   00141:  * ✅ Chave canônica para CACHE:
   00142:  * - "RJ" -> "PT_RIO"
   00143:  * - Federal (qualquer alias) -> "FEDERAL"
   00144:  * - outros -> UPPER(TRIM)
   00145:  */
   00146: function canonicalScopeKey(ufInput) {
   00147:   const s = String(ufInput || "").trim();
   00148:   if (!s) return "";
   00149:   const up = s.toUpperCase();
   00150: 
   00151:   if (up === RJ_STATE_CODE) return RJ_LOTTERY_KEY;
   00152:   if (isFederalInput(up)) return FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE;
   00153: 
   00154:   return up;
   00155: }

   01004: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
   01005:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   01006:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
   01007: 
   01008:   const parts = prizes
   01009:     .map((p) => {
   01010:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
   01011:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
   01012:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
   01013:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
   01014:         pos
   01015:       );
   01016: 
   01017:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
   01018:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
   01019:     })
   01020:     .filter(Boolean)
   01021:     .sort();
   01022: 
   01023:   return parts.join("|");
   01024: }

   01026: function federalHourPriority(draw) {
   01027:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
   01028:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
   01029:   );
   01030:   const ymd = String(draw?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) || "").trim();
   01031:   const n = hourToNumSafe(h);
   01032: 
   01033:   const prefer20h = getFederalPreferredHourForYmd(ymd) === "20:00";
   01034: 
   01035:   if (prefer20h) {
   01036:     if (h === "20:00") return 200000 + n;
   01037:     if (h === "19:00") return 190000 + n;
   01038:     return 100000 + n;
   01039:   }
   01040: 
   01041:   if (h === "19:00") return 200000 + n;
   01042:   if (h === "20:00") return 190000 + n;
   01043:   return 100000 + n;
   01044: }
   01045: 
   01046: function pickBetterFederalClone(a, b) {
   01047:   const pa = Number.isFinite(Number(a?.prizesCount))
   01048:     ? Number(a.prizesCount)
   01049:     : Array.isArray(a?.prizes)
   01050:     ? a.prizes.length
   01051:     : 0;
   01052: 
   01053:   const pb = Number.isFinite(Number(b?.prizesCount))
   01054:     ? Number(b.prizesCount)
   01055:     : Array.isArray(b?.prizes)
   01056:     ? b.prizes.length
   01057:     : 0;
   01058: 
   01059:   if (pa !== pb) return pb > pa ? b : a;
